Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. Um, hi. Thank you for seeing me. I, um... I guess I'm here because things have just been a little overwhelming lately. I mean, I know everyone gets stressed, and I don't want to make a big deal out of nothing, but... I just can't seem to shake this feeling that I'm doing everything wrong. 

Like, at work—I'm a business operations specialist—and lately, anytime there's a hiccup, I just feel like it's entirely my fault. Like I'm just a really bad employee. And it's not just work. I bought a house a while ago, and I just keep thinking I'm a terrible homeowner and I'm going to lose it all, and... and that I probably deserve to. I'm sorry, I know that sounds silly, and I'm probably just overreacting. Does that make sense? I hope I'm explaining this right.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um, well... I mean, I really try to stay on top of everything at work, I do. I have all my spreadsheets organized and I use a planner, so I try to make sure I don't miss anything important. I don't want to be the person dropping the ball, you know?

But... I guess when I'm feeling really down and I haven't been sleeping... maybe I do have to read the same email a few times before I actually understand what it says. Or I might be in a meeting and just kind of... zone out for a minute and lose track of what we were talking about. I always catch myself and try to play it off, so I don't think anyone else has really noticed! And I still get my work done. It just takes me a little longer sometimes because my brain feels a bit foggy. 

I hope that doesn't make it sound like I'm doing a bad job at work. I really am trying my hardest. Is it okay that I get a little distracted like that when I'm tired?
